## Audit findings fixed

1. **Premature remote lamp shutdown** could expire before player reaches motor.
   - New dialogue uses only a persistent camera change; explains why lamp is not shut now.
2. **Player seemingly omniscient at entrance** despite following camera.
   - Player scouts relevant route and returns to contact.
   - Robot observation is explicitly **“刚才”**, not claimed current.
3. **Two interventions lacked distinct reasons**.
   - Example deliberately uses one modification and explains its tradeoff.
4. **Overuse of codes**.
   - Main prose/table code occurrences reduced **21 → 2**, both the same camera ID **C4** used to explain interface targeting.
   - Count excludes image paths, versions, coordinates.
   - Record: `tmp/pdfs/preliminary_v04/editorial_counts.json`.
5. **“系统” confused enemy Agent with deterministic engine**.
   - Fixed terms: **城市系统** vs **游戏程序**.
6. **Fixed shape did not constrain guess area**.
   - Proposal now says size is constrained by program; exact shape/size remains undecided.
   - 5×5 is explicitly illustrative.
7. **Lighting and scanning ambiguities**.
   - Turning off one lamp does not darken a tile still lit by another.
   - Sweep sector is different from instantaneous FOV.
8. **Quota wording implied banking across contacts**.
   - Each contact independent; no invented global intervention pool or transfer.
9. **External Agent conversation limits overstated**.
   - Game panel limits messages; interface limits effective commands during authorized contact.
   - Cannot force-count all chatting in outside software.
10. **Snapshot omitted added facility instances**.
    - Device list is part of restored physical state.
    - Next-round initialization specifics remain undecided.
11. **Rollback vs defeat flowchart unclear**.
    - Now explicit decision “是否使用回退”, with separate restore/failure branches.
12. **10-minute budget ambiguous**.
    - Whole four-round experience includes dialogue, model wait, rollbacks.
    - First development phase now tests both model calls early.
13. **Old proposals mixed with current rules**.
    - Archived old mechanism document; current v0.20 cleaned up.
    - Old story’s permanent help counter, threshold ending, file-deletion boss are not current mechanics.

## Settled mechanics to preserve

- High overhead 2.5D, flat square grid, continuous real-time movement.
- No jump, stairs, height differences, chasing, autonomous repair, or instant capture.
- Passive lamps/cameras/robots.
- Binary illumination; reliable observation requires light, FOV, unobstructed.
- Reports **有人 / 无人 / 无法确定**, actual observation timestamps.
- Continuous sensing, periodic batches, fixed batch count per localization.
- At localization, whole world pauses; enemy guesses an area; **program checks frozen true position**.
- Only localization hit triggers rollback choice/round failure.
- Miss permits system changes and one additional facility; legal slots/cap/types remain proposals.
- Personal assistant cannot see map/live screen/spatial distribution; player descriptions supply context.
- Assistant first version proposed lights and camera controls, **not robot or report falsification**.
- Contact dialogue pauses all game clocks, devices, cooldowns, reports.
- System inference/contact mutually exclusive.
- System commands only after localization miss; assistant commands at end of contact.
- Same device/control field: last valid command wins, persists across cycles unless overwritten/hard rule/reset.
- Each contact once per complete round, each conversation max **1–2 modifications**; exact choice/count details undecided.
- Local rollback restores contact used/unused flags from snapshot.
- Must reach usable contact again; old commands not auto-resubmitted.
- Repair motor and reach exit = round success. Repair reports real events.
- System **3 whole-round resets**, initial + 3 = **4 rounds**.
- Player **3 local rollbacks shared across whole game**, current localization-cycle start.
- Hit → rollback or round defeat; later rounds continue; final overall evaluation.
- Physical states, devices including additions, clocks, motor progress, effective reports, contact flags restore.
- Remaining rollback count and settled scores do not restore.
- Player, assistant, city system retain **their own legitimately received memories**.
- History must not become current observations or grant extra coordinates.
- Candidate map **36×24**, four interconnected zones, 3 contacts, 1 motor, 4 cameras, 6 lamps, 2 robots.
- These counts/layout and 5×5 example are not validated balance commitments.

## Important unresolved items, honestly retained

The audit does **not** claim text fixes prove:
- Agent dialogue is strategically worthwhile compared with direct controls.
- More requests necessarily cause failure.
- Larger map/repair timings are balanced.
- Four rounds with model delays fit 10 minutes.

Still require prototype tests or decisions:
- Localization size/shape, report cadence.
- Repair duration/event granularity.
- Lamp duration/cooldown.
- New facility slots/types/cap.
- Exact dialogue/command limits.
- Single-round time limit.
- Model timeout/invalid-output behavior.

Do not invent punishments, global currency, timeout defeat, or fallback rules to close these silently.

逐项对应见[完整核对记录](D:/djh/search_agent/tms_optimizer/hackathon/output/预赛模板符合性复核_2026-11-15.md)。

## 二、优先补充什么

### 1. 信息卡：提交前必须补齐

需要王嘉璐、董骏豪各自的：

- 邮箱、手机号。
- 在项目中实际负责的工作。

评分表还关注团队能力匹配，建议每人补 **1–2句真实相关经验**。作品链接选填，但官方强烈建议提供。[信息卡及评分要求](D:/djh/search_agent/tms_optimizer/hackathon/tmp/preliminary_20261115/requirements/template_all_blocks.txt)

### 2. 赛题理解：说明“我们想改善什么体验”

现稿故事讲清楚了，但创作动机偏弱。建议补一小段，例如：

> 我们希望让潜入游戏的重试需要重新观察和判断。城市系统会依据上一轮收到的报告调整监控，玩家则通过描述现场，与助手讨论新的办法，再亲手执行。

这是拟解决的体验问题，不能写成已经经过用户研究证明的结论。

### 3. AI接入：把实际体验入口说清楚

现稿讲清了助手的权限，还需交代：

- 玩家在哪个客户端加载随游戏提供的 skill？
- 到联络点后，如何把对话结果提交给游戏？
- 城市系统如何接收报告、返回定位及调度？
- 模型超时或返回无效指令时，怎样结束等待？

补几句话即可，预赛无需展开完整架构。我的建议是先确定一种演示接入方式，避免同时承诺多种入口。

### 4. RESET：增加一个前后变化的例子

例如：

> 上轮南侧通路出现多次目击；重启后，城市系统将摄像头扫描区间转向那里。玩家发现变化，在联络点重新描述，助手结合之前的经历比较新路线。

这能具体说明**记忆如何改变下一次体验**。应标为演示设想，且城市系统只能使用它真正收到的信息。

### 5. 可行性：补上十分钟如何容纳四轮

现稿已有72小时安排，但四轮行动、联络、模型等待、回退如何合计约十分钟，还缺预算。

另外可补一句预期制作价值：**复用一张地图和现有设备，通过有限配置变化呈现多轮挑战**。无需虚构节省成本的百分比。

以上属于加强方案说服力的建议，具体示例已写入[核对记录](D:/djh/search_agent/tms_optimizer/hackathon/output/预赛模板符合性复核_2026-11-15.md)。

## 三、不需要继续加的材料

模板**不要求预赛提供 Demo、代码、完整立项或架构文档，也未限定页数**。自由展示区可选；互动剧的世界观图与角色图要求，不应套到我们的游戏赛道。[模板说明](D:/djh/search_agent/tms_optimizer/hackathon/tmp/preliminary_20261115/requirements/template_all_blocks.txt)

**下一步优先补信息卡，再补体验问题、AI接入和跨轮变化三小段。现阶段可以把材料完善起来，无需继续扩大玩法规模。**
